{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_vrtx_011ChEmA6pEKBoySSH7JHy5b","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I fixed four layout bugs in bokehjs. One caveat first: `recording.mp4` wasn't there (`/app/.harbor_assets/` is empty), so I couldn't watch the clip. I found the bugs by reading the layout code against your expected behaviour, then checked the fixes in headless Chromium.\n\n**Fixes**\n1. **Frames lined up against the wrong column** (`models/layouts/alignments.ts`, `align_borders`): the left border was taken from the end column and the right border from the start column. A chart spanning several grid columns got the wrong borders, so its plot area shifted and labels could be cut off. Each side now uses its own column.\n2. **Top border ignored when lining up frames** (`core/layout/border.ts`): the left, right and bottom borders allowed for the extra space added to line frames up, but the top didn't. A chart with a fixed-size plot area could then lose part of its title or toolbar at the top. The top now works like the other three sides.\n3. **Row and Column skipped border alignment** (`models/layouts/flex_box.ts`): only GridBox ran the step that grows each chart's borders to match its neighbours. In a Row or Column, lining up the plot areas could cut off axis labels or push a chart past its slot. Row and Column now run the same step.\n4. **No relayout when labels got bigger** (`core/layout/side_panel.ts`, `has_size_changed`): the check was reversed, so a relayout fired only when an axis or label panel shrank. When tick labels grew, for example `3` becoming `5.000e+9` after a zoom, they were clipped. It now triggers when the content no longer fits.\n\n**How I checked**\nThe build and eslint pass. I didn't run the unit test suite. I built test pages with three charts that have fixed plot areas but different titles, toolbars and axis labels, and measured them before and after a zoom that lengthens the tick labels:\n- **Row:** all three charts are 202px tall, the plot areas share a top edge, and each stays 200×150.\n- **Column:** all three charts are 331px wide and the plot areas share a left edge.\n- **GridBox with one chart spanning two columns:** its plot area lines up with the chart above it, and nothing overflows the grid.\n- **After the zoom:** the zoomed chart widens its axis panel from 52px to 83px so the labels stay whole, and the charts sharing its column follow.\n\nScreenshots show every label and toolbar whole, with no overlaps."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":507,"cache_read_input_tokens":77163,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":507,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"48126d38-6881-41e0-8c1c-1a5430dea66a","uuid":"92bd227f-1f5d-4d5d-8630-d945f61c6f39","timestamp":"2026-11-20T19:51:16.097Z"}
